Abstract: Dexterous grasp synthesis has advanced rapidly in generating stable and physically plausible hand poses, but real-world manipulation requires grasps that preserve the function implied by the task. We study open-vocabulary task-oriented dexterous grasp generation, where a robot must infer functional intent from free-form language, ground it in multi-view visual observations and object geometry, and generate an executable high-degree-of-freedom grasp. We present OpenDexGrasp, a unified data and generative modeling framework for this setting. OpenDexVerse provides dual-source supervision organized by the Coverage-to-Alignment (C2A) Recipe: OpenDex-Scale offers large-scale semantic and geometric coverage through automatic grasp synthesis and vision-language annotation, while OpenDex-Align supplies high-quality embodied alignment through human teleoperation and category-level transfer. OpenDexGrasp learns a shared perception-action latent representation that couples open-vocabulary vision-language context with dexterous action generation. Affordance grounding and grasp generation provide complementary supervision over this latent space, enabling direct generation of task-consistent dexterous grasps without a separate affordance-to-pose inference stage. Extensive simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate improved functional alignment, physical feasibility, generalization to unseen categories, and real-world execution success. Additional details and videos are available at https://opendexgrasp.github.io/.

</details>


### [219] [Fetch My Beer: Synthetic-to-real Hierarchical Policy for Smooth Pick-and-place](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18119)
*Yingyue Li,Chenyangguang Zhang,Ruida Zhang,Bowen Fu,Guangyao Zhai,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于机器人稳定运输液体容器的新框架，可以有效抑制液体晃动和防止溢出，显著提升运动平顺性和动态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作主要关注完成任务，如搬运目标到指定位置，但对于液体容器等动态敏感操作，容易导致晃动和溢出问题。传统的强化学习无法高效在线模拟流体，模仿学习中遥操作也会带来非预期波动，现有策略更关注任务完成而非动态稳定性，因此亟需能兼顾效率与动态控制的新方法。

Method: 提出一种合成到现实的框架：1）结合物理验证的数据生成流程，自动合成抓取动作，通过视觉-语言模型筛选不稳定的姿态，再结合流体仿真验证运输轨迹的有效性；2）控制策略方面，采用分层设计，高层将语言和视觉观察翻译为SE(3)控制目标，底层采用基于扩散模型的控制器，先在紧凑的潜空间高效规划，再解码出高频率动作，保证运动流畅和动态稳定。

Result: 大量实验表明，该系统在运输平稳性和动态稳定性方面优于当前最先进的操作策略。

Conclusion: 结合物理仿真与分层扩散控制，本方法有效解决了液体运输时的动态稳定问题，为机器人处理更复杂动态任务开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Many real-world robotic applications require dynamically sensitive manipulation, where success depends not only on reaching a target state but on maintaining stable object dynamics throughout execution. We study the stable transport of liquid-filled containers, where a robot must move objects to target locations while suppressing sloshing and preventing spillage. Unlike conventional pick-and-place, this task imposes stringent requirements on motion smoothness and trajectory-level stability, exposing clear limitations in existing systems. Specifically, fluid simulation remains too costly for online reinforcement learning; human teleoperation introduces unintended accelerations that induce sloshing during imitation learning; and current policy pipelines optimize for task completion rather than dynamic stability. We propose a synthetic-to-real framework coupling physically validated data generation with a hierarchical, diffusion-based controller. The scalable data pipeline synthesizes grasps, filters unstable poses via a vision-language model, and validates transport trajectories through fluid simulation. The policy is organized with a high-level module that translates language and visual observations into SE(3) control targets, and a latent diffusion controller that first plans efficiently in a compact latent space and then decodes dense action chunks, enabling the high control frequency needed for smooth and stable motion. Extensive experiments show our system outperforms state-of-the-art manipulation policies in transport smoothness and dynamic stability. Our project page: https://fetch-my-beer.github.io/

</details>


### [220] [Prior Evolution and Task Alignment for Aerial Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18153)
*Weiliang Deng,Zhengyang Dang,Yao Mu,Ximin Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合学习的方法，提升无人机在空中抓取任务中的轨迹优化鲁棒性与性能。通过学习先验和引入执行感知评价，显著改善了优化效率与实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受猛禽空中抓取能力启发，目前的轨迹优化方法在人为设计目标和优化非凸性上受限，难以获得高质量解。因此，需引入数据驱动的学习机制提升优化过程与抓取可靠性。

Method: 首先，从优化轨迹中学习轨迹先验，并通过基于交叉熵算法（CEM）的过程不断进化先验以提升初值质量。其次，引入执行感知评价器，依据实际抓取效果学习以评估轨迹，且其评价作为可微分的代价函数用于辅助优化。

Result: 通过仿真及真实环境实验，所提方法在优化稳定性、轨迹一致性和抓取成功率等方面均优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 结合学习的轨迹优化方法可有效克服传统空中抓取轨迹规划中的非凸性和目标建模局限性，提升系统抓取能力。

Abstract: Aerial grasping is a remarkable capability exhibited by predatory birds, allowing them to capture prey through highly coordinated maneuvers in flight. Inspired by this capability, researchers have developed various formulations to reproduce such maneuvers through trajectory optimization. However, two limitations remain in practice. First, the resulting optimization problem is highly nonconvex and sensitive to initialization, making high-quality solutions difficult to obtain under a limited computational budget. Second, prescribed numerical objectives are human-designed abstractions that describe successful grasping through a limited set of mathematically tractable quantities and may not fully capture what determines task success. We investigate how learning can address these limitations within an analytical planner. Accordingly, a trajectory prior is first learned from optimized motions and then evolved through a CEM-based process that evaluates sampled initializations with the deployed optimizer and retains favorable ones as new supervision. An Execution-Aware Critic learns from contact, lift, and completion outcomes to assess whether the optimized trajectories are likely to succeed in physical execution. Its frozen energy can further serve as a differentiable grasping cost, allowing execution data to directly shape trajectory generation. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate improved optimization reliability, trajectory consistency, and grasping performance.

</details>


### [221] [Energy-Regularized Imitation Learning for Force- and Work-Aware Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18164)
*Toshiki Otani,Hiromu Taketsugu,Norimichi Ukita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合能耗（机械功）优化与策略微调的机器人操作学习方法，通过引入可微能量预测器提升机器人的操作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略未充分考虑机械能耗，导致实际应用中常出现运动效率低下的问题。因此，作者希望开发一种能够约束能耗的优化方式，实现高效的机器人操作。

Method: 首先，作者基于机器人关节力矩与角位移定义了机械功的近似值，并设计了一个可微分的能量预测器来估算该机械功。利用该预测器，将非可微的物理量转化为可微正则项，从而在不依赖显式动力学模型的前提下，对预训练的操作策略进行精细化微调。随后，作者在RLBench基准和RVT-2平台上执行包括接触、运动、放置、推动、扫掠等12类操作任务进行验证。

Result: 经过能耗约束的微调后，机器人操作的平均机械功从208.8J降低到204.4J，降低了2.1%；与此同时，任务成功率从86.2%略升至86.9%。

Conclusion: 在无须显式可微动力学模型的前提下，结合能耗约束的策略优化不仅提升了操作效率，还保持甚至略提升了任务表现，展现了机械功感知优化在机器人操作学习中的应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper studies energy-aware manipulation as a physically grounded learning problem. We define a joint-space mechanical-work proxy from joint torque and angular displacement, and train a differentiable energy predictor that estimates this work from robot states and actions. The predictor converts a non-differentiable simulator-side physical quantity into a differentiable regularizer for fine-tuning a pretrained manipulation policy. We instantiate the framework with RVT-2 on RLBench and evaluate 12 manipulation tasks involving object contact, articulated motion, placement, pushing, and sweeping. The proposed fine-tuning reduces the average mechanical work from 208.8J to 204.4J (i.e., 2.1% reduction), while the mean task success rate also increases slightly from 86.2% to 86.9%. These results show that work-aware policy optimization can suppress physically inefficient motion without requiring an explicit differentiable dynamics model.

</details>


### [222] [LUMO: Designing Luminous Contact Morphology for Repeatable Whole-Finger Contact Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18165)
*Dong Ho Kang,Youngsu Ko,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型低阻抗机器人手指，通过与嵌入式LED和硅胶垫相结合的结构，使得手指在受力时不仅能测量关节力矩，还能推断出受力点在手指上的具体位置。该设计经过机械和光学双重优化，实现了更精确的力感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的机器人手指只能通过关节力矩估算受力大小，但无法准确确定受力点的位置，而不同位置的受力对于机器手操作物体的稳定性和灵巧性十分关键，因此需要一种能够直接感知接触点位置的方法。

Method: 采用集成LED照明和可变形硅胶垫的手指结构，接触时变形导致不同位置的光分布发生特定变化；优化结构截面（包括垫型面、承载件、侧向孔），同时兼顾力学和光学性能，并通过刚-软接触仿真、光线追踪和多目标贝叶斯优化进行参数搜索。

Result: 在两种硅胶、六个接触点、两种球形压入器上测试，优化后的结构能够更好地区分相邻位置，提升了15%-59%的分辨能力。通过光学响应和已知LED间距估算接触位置，再结合关节力矩，可实现平均绝对误差为1.44N的法向力估计。在两指抓取实验中，多个手指节点同时出现局部光学响应。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构可实现机器人手指对接触位置的外显感知，大幅提升了触觉分辨率和力估计精度，为多指机器人精准抓取和操作提供了新型传感能力。

Abstract: A low-impedance robot finger reports through joint torque how strongly it is loaded, but the same torque can arise from a small force near the fingertip or a large force near the joint. Resolving the force therefore requires knowing where along the finger contact occurred. LUMO makes that location externally observable. Embedded LEDs illuminate a compliant silicone pad, and contact deforms the pad so that light emerging from the finger's side changes in a pattern set by where the load acts. Because the same structure also carries the contact load, we optimize its cross-section, including the pad profile, rigid carrier, and lateral void, for two behaviors at once. Mechanically, the pad conforms under low preload while the carrier increasingly restricts further deformation as load rises. Optically, different contact locations produce separated responses on the finger's side. The search uses rigid--soft contact simulation, ray tracing, and multi-objective Bayesian optimization. Across two silicones, six contact locations, and 10- and 30-mm spherical indenters, the optimized morphologies improve neighboring-location separation relative to variation from re-establishing contact by \(15\)--\(59\%\). Estimating contact location from the optical response using the known LED spacing and combining it with joint torque gives \(1.44~\mathrm{N}\) normal-force MAE over 931 samples. In a two-finger hand, localized side responses appear on several links simultaneously during grasps.

</details>


### [223] [Characterizing Replay Retention Under Dynamics Shift in Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18167)
*Everest Yang,Skye Thompson,George D. Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在持续性模型强化学习中，机器人动力学变化时选择重放数据（经验回放）策略的问题，提出动态判断何时应该遗忘陈旧数据以平衡适应性和数据有效性的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人动力学会发生变化，然而在持续性强化学习中，如何利用历史经验来加速适应新环境，却不被过时经验拖累，是一个关键难题。如果直接丢弃历史数据可能导致数据匮乏，尤其在原有动力学回归时错失利用旧数据的机会，因此需要精细的经验管理策略。

Method: 作者提出利用两个指标——变化幅度（change magnitude）以及经验的“年龄-陈旧度曲线下的面积”（AUC），来判断当前环境下新旧经验的保留与丢弃策略。通过在两种机器人形态、两种模型型强化学习算法和现实世界RL基准变化下，测试这些策略的效果，并尝试用交互数据估算上述指标，以指导实际应用中经验回放的管理。

Result: 实验证明，在动力学发生巨大且永久性变化时，忘却陈旧数据有助于模型适应新环境，但如果动力学有可能回归原状，则过早丢弃旧数据反而有害。数据陈旧度的估算可以一定程度上辅助判别何时应当使用历史数据。

Conclusion: 经验回放策略的优选依赖于动力学变化的幅度及其演化趋势。需要动态估计数据陈旧度以做出灵活策略选择，从而在适应性和数据利用率之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Adapting to changes in robot dynamics requires learning from new data without discarding experience that may still be useful. In continual model-based reinforcement learning (RL), replay collected before a dynamics change can slow adaptation, while removing it unnecessarily reduces available training data and can be especially costly if earlier dynamics return. We study when recent transitions are preferable to the full replay history. Two quantities characterize this trade-off: change magnitude and age-staleness area under the curve (AUC), measuring how well transition age separates stale from fresh data. Forgetting stale data helps after large permanent shifts but hurts when dynamics recur and older data becomes useful again. Choosing a replay strategy therefore depends on predicting when older data will help or hurt. We test these effects across two locomotion morphologies, two model-based RL algorithms, and Real-World RL benchmark perturbations. Because ground-truth staleness labels are unavailable on deployed robots, we evaluate whether an estimator built from interaction data can still provide the quantities needed to choose a replay strategy after permanent changes. Our results show that replay retention depends on change magnitude and on how the dynamics evolve.

</details>


### [224] [Approximating High Dimensional Self-Motion Manifolds via Deep Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18169)
*Haitao Gao,Yang Song,Liao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率分布的方法，可高效恢复高维冗余机械臂在给定末端位姿下的自运动流形（SMM），突破了现有方法仅适用于低维曲线流形的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主流自运动流形恢复方法都假定SMM为一维曲线，难以处理更高维（高度冗余）情形，限制了机械臂全局最优运动规划能力。为此，亟需一种适用于高维SMM的新方法。

Method: 该文将SMM视为给定末端位姿下机械臂构型条件后验分布的支撑集，将SMM恢复问题转化为对该分布的采样并通过聚类分离其不连通分支。该方法与流形维度或冗余度无关，不需网络结构修改。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在一维SMM问题（如最新null-space和学习法）上取得相当表现，并首次能够成功近似7R机械臂中4维高度冗余的SMM。

Conclusion: 作者提出的方法突破了SMM维度的限制，为高冗余机械臂运动规划提供了强有力的工具，并具有良好的扩展性，为后续更复杂机械臂系统的流形学习与运动规划铺平了道路。

Abstract: Self-motion manifold (SMM) characterizes the geometric structure of the infinite inverse kinematic solutions set of a redundant manipulator at a fixed end-effector pose, and its efficient recovery underpins feasible and global optimal motion planning. Existing methods such as null-space continuation and learning-based methods are formulated around the assumption that an SMM is a curve, and do not extend to higher redundancy orders. We instead adopt a probabilistic view: SMMs are the support of the conditional posterior over configurations given a target pose, so that recovering it reduces to sampling from a learned distribution and separating its disjoint components by clustering. The formulation is independent of the manifold dimension and requires no architectural change as the redundancy order grows. In this work, we demonstrate that our method can approximate 1-D SMMs with performance comparable to the latest null-space continuation and learning-based approach, and that it is the first method capable of approximating highly redundant 4-D SMMs in a 7R manipulator for position tasks. Project website: \href{https://github.com/accuracy-maker/high-dimenstional-self-motion-manifold-approximation}{https://github.com/accuracy-maker/high-dimenstional-self-motion-manifold-approximation}

</details>


### [225] [TacBPM: A Tactile-conditioned Behavior Prior Model for Dexterous Reorientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18174)
*Jie Yin,Wanli Xing,Zeyuan Zhao,Xuezhou Zhu,Zhijie Deng,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于触觉-本体感受历史条件的行为先验模型，以提升多自由度机械手灵巧操控的能力，并验证了其在仿真和实际中的泛化和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多自由度机械手在进行物体灵巧操控时，需要同时协调高维关节与复杂的接触交互，解决目标朝向追踪、稳定性与适应性等难题，现有方法训练效率低且在泛化和稳定性上存在不足。

Method: 作者提出了一种触觉条件的行为先验模型，训练过程中将多尺度的球状专家行为提炼为潜在控制器，下游策略通过残差潜在动作复用这一固定的触觉先验，从而减少从原始关节命令重新探索的需求。行为先验条件于机械手与物体的触觉和本体感受历史，使策略动态反映当前互动状态。

Result: 方法在多种任务（异向各向异性物体姿态转移、指定轴旋转、以及抓取到任意姿态等）中进行了评估，实验表明该方法可加速训练过程，实现更稳定的策略，与传统PPO直接输出关节命令的基线相比有显著提升，并实现了良好的仿真到实物迁移。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了机械手灵巧操控策略的泛化性与稳定性，在实际任务中表现出色，为复杂操作场景中的高效智能控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires policies that coordinate high-DoF hand joints through intermittent, contact-rich interaction. Beyond target-orientation tracking, such policies must discover finger gaits that preserve object stability while adapting to geometry, anisotropy, pose, contact, and sensing changes. We propose \method, a tactile-conditioned behavior prior model for dexterous reorientation. \method distills multi-scale sphere specialists into a latent controller and lets downstream policies reuse the fixed tactile prior through residual latent actions, reducing renewed exploration from raw joint commands. The prior conditions on tactile-proprioceptive history so latent behavior reflects the current hand-object interaction. We evaluate arbitrary-pose transfer across anisotropic objects, commanded-axis rotation, and an arm-hand Grasp-to-AnyPose task in which the robot must grasp, lift, transport, and reach goal poses for novel tool geometries and generalized placements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method accelerates training and enables stable policies where matched raw-action PPO remains near failure, with successful sim-to-real transfer in in-hand and arm-hand tasks.

</details>


### [226] [OmniRisk: Omnidirectional Trajectory-Risk Learning for Agile Quadrotor Dynamic Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18191)
*Yifan He,Yang Liu,Wenhao Zhao,Hai Lin,Deping Zhang,Mingze Ma,Fei Gao,Huan Yu,Zipeng Dai,Ziming Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了OmniRisk，一个针对四旋翼在高速动态障碍环境下规避障碍的高效全向风险推理与轨迹规划框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，四旋翼需要在障碍物快速接近时迅速反应并规避，但现有方法要么计算量大，不适合高密度障碍，要么高效但预测不可靠。主要难点在于稀疏的传感器数据难以准确预测障碍物运动，同时在线轨迹优化随障碍物数量增加而计算量爆炸。

Method: OmniRisk通过离线学习轨迹层面的风险评估来高效实现四旋翼避障。采用固定维度张量融合LiDAR全景、动态遮罩和笛卡尔表面速度信息，将障碍物的几何和运动特征联合建模。定义了与障碍速度对齐的非对称风险场，对接近（高风险）和远离（低风险）情况加权不同。用累积风险密集监督轨迹，避免避障时不必要的迟疑。设计了双分支环形卷积网络，一次前向推理即可预测所有候选轨迹的风险和边界状态，然后选取最优动作并解析重建轨迹。

Result: OmniRisk避免了对每条轨迹进行逐条风险计算，规划效率与障碍物数量无关。在真实飞行中，系统可在相对速度高达15 m/s情况下连续成功完成避险，无需再进行任务相关的微调。

Conclusion: OmniRisk实现了高效、鲁棒的高动态障碍规避，适用于实际无人机应用，并在高难度场景中表现优异，有潜力推广到更多机器人领域。

Abstract: Agile quadrotor avoidance of fast-moving obstacles requires anticipating collisions and selecting feasible maneuvers within short reaction windows. Reliable predictive avoidance remains challenging because sparse range observations do not directly reveal obstacle motion, while online trajectory optimizers either scale poorly with obstacle count or remain efficient at the expense of reliability in dense, high-speed encounters. We present OmniRisk, an omnidirectional planning framework that learns trajectory-level risk offline for efficient onboard evasion. A fixed-dimensional tensor combines LiDAR range panoramas, dynamic masks, and Cartesian surface velocities to represent geometry and motion jointly. We formulate an asymmetric risk field aligned with obstacle velocity that emphasizes approaching interactions and attenuates receding ones. Accumulating this risk along predicted relative trajectories provides dense supervision and discourages unnecessary hesitation after obstacles pass. A dual-branch circular convolutional network predicts terminal boundary states and dynamic risks for candidate primitives over an omnidirectional anchor lattice in a single forward pass, followed by selection and closed-form reconstruction of the selected candidate primitive. This formulation removes online risk accumulation along trajectories and makes risk-inference cost independent of obstacle count. OmniRisk enables efficient onboard avoidance, with real-world flights demonstrating consecutive evasive maneuvers at relative encounter speeds up to 15 m/s without fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/VANdexj/OmniRisk.

</details>


### [227] [WAVE-Go: World-Model Navigation with Adaptive Execution for Wheel-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18193)
*Mingyi Li,Ji Li,Zhihao Ouyang,Yage He,Börje F. Karlsson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了WAVE-Go，这是一个专为轮腿机器人设计的图像目标导航框架，实现了可中断的自适应命令执行和行动前缀选择方法，以应对动态障碍和行走模式变化带来的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽然能预测导航动作后果，但在机器人实际行动时容易因环境变化（如动态障碍或模式切换）而导致原先规划失效，因此需要一种更为灵活、能随时中断并重新规划的导航执行机制。

Method: WAVE-Go将世界-行动预测与命令执行相分离。其执行器根据最新观测自适应选择行动前缀，并在原命令失效时中断待执行命令。通过条件风险形式化，结合状态、行走模式切换时的安全、稳定和任务证据检查，确保执行合理。

Result: 在导航评测中，WAVE-Go在分布内和动态分布外的成功率分别为74.1%和63.3%，较最强基线提升4.7和7.7个百分点，碰撞次数由每百米4.4降至2.9。与固定命令数的可中断基线相比，成功率提升4.0个百分点，重规划频率降低51.2%，碰撞率下降6.5%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，可自适应、可中断的执行机制有效提升了导航表现并降低规划成本，是提升机器人导航能力的有效手段。

Abstract: World models can anticipate the consequences of navigation actions, but predicted action sequences may become invalid during execution, especially when wheel-legged robots encounter dynamic obstacles or change locomotion modes. We propose WAVE-Go, an image-goal navigation framework that separates world-action prediction from interruptible command execution. Its executor adaptively selects an action prefix and cancels pending commands when updated observations invalidate execution. A conditional-risk formulation specifies prefix selection under an estimated cumulative failure budget, while posture and locomotion-mode transitions require clearance, stability, and task-evidence checks. In the reported navigation evaluation, WAVE-Go achieves 74.1% in-distribution success and 63.3% dynamic out-of-distribution success, exceeding the strongest baseline by 4.7 and 7.7 percentage points, respectively, while reducing collisions from 4.4 to 2.9 per 100 m. Compared with interruptible fixed four-command execution, WAVE-Go raises success by 4.0 percentage points while reducing replanning frequency by 51.2% and collision rate by 6.5%. Execution ablations also show that runtime interruption improves success, collision rate, and reaction latency at the cost of additional replanning. These results support adaptive, interruptible execution as a means of balancing navigation performance and planning overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/vigorlee/wave-go.

</details>


### [228] [WholeBodyWAM: Learning Whole-Body World Action Models with Scalable Motion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18197)
*Bowei Zhang,Qiyao Zhang,Shuanghao Bai,Xinhua Wang,Meng Li,Yilei Wang,Leiwang Zhang,Jian Tang,Lu Zhou,Lei Sun,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种面向人形机器人全身运动任务的世界-动作建模方法 WholeBodyWAM，旨在利用大规模但异构的人类和机器人运动数据作为模型的预训练先验，提高目标机器人的学习效率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练人形机器人进行全身操控任务通常需要大量针对性运动数据，采集成本高且难以扩展，而来自人类及其他人形机器人的全身运动数据却相对丰富。论文探讨如何将这些易获取的运动资源转化为可迁移的世界动作建模先验，从而减少目标机器人数据需求。

Method: 1) 建立了UniMotion-4K大规模异构运动库，统一来自人类视频、3D运动数据和不同人形平台的4K小时动作；2) 运用语言引导的Motion Expert无监督预训练未来全身运动预测；3) 在机器人后续自适应训练中，通过不对称MoT机制整合视频、动作和运动专家信息，实现运动知识迁移和动作生成。

Result: 实验证明，WholeBodyWAM随着运动预训练规模扩大表现提升，能够更好预测未来动作并提升特定任务表现，实现全身操控向现实机器人任务的迁移，且在有限机器人演示数据下也展现出极高数据效率。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，先利用大规模异构运动数据预训练再微调到目标机器人场景，可有效增强人形机器人全身操控任务的数据利用率和泛化表现，对提升机器人学习效率具有重要意义。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation requires coordinated whole-body dynamics, yet large-scale trajectories from a target robot are expensive to collect and difficult to scale. In contrast, whole-body motion from human and humanoid sources is abundantly available, although such data cannot be directly used as embodiment-specific robot actions. This work asks whether these scalable motion resources can instead provide a transferable predictive prior for humanoid world-action modeling. We introduce WholeBodyWAM, a humanoid world-action model that learns whole-body dynamics from large-scale heterogeneous motion before target-robot training. We curate UniMotion-4K, a motion corpus spanning more than 4K hours from human videos, native 3D motion datasets, and heterogeneous humanoid platforms, and canonicalize these diverse sources into a unified motion space. A language-conditioned Motion Expert is then pretrained to predict future whole-body motion without target-robot action supervision. During robot post-training, the pretrained Motion Expert is integrated with Video and Action Experts through asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention, enabling predictive scene dynamics and whole-body motion to jointly inform embodiment-specific action generation. Experiments show that WholeBodyWAM consistently benefits from increased motion-pretraining scale, improves future-motion prediction and downstream task performance, and transfers effectively to real-world humanoid manipulation. Moreover, the pretrained motion prior substantially improves data efficiency under limited target-robot demonstrations.

</details>


### [229] [Reinforcement Learning for Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18207)
*Perry Dong,Kuo-Han Hung,Dorsa Sadigh,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的实时强化学习微调方法Real-Time EXPO-FT，有效提升了大规模视觉-语言-动作模型在动态机器人任务中的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推理延迟高，导致机器人执行时观察信息滞后，分布偏移严重降低了表现和可靠性，现有多为模仿学习方案，无法突破训练分布限制。作者希望实现在高效、可靠基础上，满足真实世界动态任务的实时强化学习微调。

Method: 基于EXPO-FT框架，将慢速但表达能力强的预训练VLA模型提出动作块，结合轻量级的编辑策略对动作进行基于最新观测的快速实时编辑，平衡大模型行为先验与实时反应。

Result: 在Kinetix基准中，Real-Time EXPO-FT使得含时延策略在10个环境中均取得最优，并在4项真实机器人动态任务上，在10分钟在线数据内平均表现由42%提升至97%，无需人工干预。

Conclusion: Real-Time EXPO-FT突破了VLA模型时延困境，实现了高效、快速适应复杂动态环境的实时强化学习微调，为机器人的可靠部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning fine-tuning on top of large, pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offers promise for highly reliable robot deployment. However, because of their scale, modern VLA models suffer from high inference latency, so the observation used to select an action is often stale by execution time, creating a distribution shift that can substantially degrade reliability and performance. Prior work has explored asynchronous policy execution to reduce the effect of latency, but these methods are mostly built on imitation learning and offer no mechanism for moving beyond the training distribution toward higher reliability. We close this gap by enabling RL fine-tuning that meets the real-time control requirements of dynamic real-world manipulation. Our approach builds on EXPO-FT, a framework for sample-efficient, reliable VLA fine-tuning with reinforcement learning, and decouples slow, expressive action generation from fast, reactive action edits: a large pretrained VLA proposes action chunks using its strong behavior prior, while a lightweight edit policy performs fast, reactive decision-making by editing actions in response to changes in state, conditioned on the latest observation. We instantiate this as Real-Time EXPO-FT, an RL framework for finetuning real-time VLA policies. On the Kinetix benchmark, Real-Time EXPO-FT enables a delayed policy to achieve the best performance among delayed and non-delayed methods in 10 out of 10 environments. On four dynamic real-world tasks, robot object passing, ball balancing, table soccer kicking, and dynamic object picking, with online robot data capped at 10 minutes, Real-Time EXPO-FT improves average policy performance from 42% to 97%, all without human intervention, demonstrating rapid, sample-efficient adaptation to challenging real-world dynamics. Website: https://pd-perry.github.io/real-time-expo-ft

</details>


### [230] [CANTABILE: Learning Expressive Dynamics for Robotic Piano Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18213)
*Woosik Kim,Wonhyeok Choi,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CANTABILE框架，实现了更具表现力的机器人钢琴演奏，不仅关注音高和节奏，还精确控制力度，显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器人钢琴演奏评价主要集中在音符准确性（即音高和起始时刻），却忽略了音乐表现性中至关重要的力度等动态参数，因此缺乏对真实音乐表现的充分反映。

Method: CANTABILE通过三个方面提升机器人钢琴演奏：1）策略在执行时引入力度目标，准确映射按键角速度至MIDI力度值，闭环音谱到触键；2）奖励函数同时关注力度复现和音符覆盖率，避免为提高力度准确性而漏弹难点音符；3）在动态感知基线策略基础上，通过指尖残差局部调整，实现更精细的力度适应性。

Result: 在含丰富力度变化的EXPRESSIVE-51数据集实验中，CANTABILE将综合评估力度、音高、起始时刻的Velocity F1分数从0.06提升到0.34，超越基线，全部51首乐曲表现提升，力度误差减半，音频距离参考录音缩小8%。随机化训练还允许运行时调整表现强度，无需重训练。

Conclusion: CANTABILE显著提升了机器人钢琴演奏的表现力和真实感，为后续更具表现力的自主音乐表演奠定技术基础。

Abstract: Robotic piano playing has emerged as a standard benchmark for dexterous bimanual manipulation, yet progress on it has been measured almost entirely by note accuracy -- which keys are pressed (pitch) and when (onset) -- leaving the musical dynamics essential for expressive performance neither rewarded nor evaluated. We propose CANTABILE, a dynamics-aware framework for robotic piano performance that (i) closes the score-to-contact loop by conditioning the policy on upcoming velocity goals and mapping each key's angular velocity at onset back to MIDI velocity, (ii) couples a velocity-fidelity reward with an onset-coverage reward, so that dynamics cannot be improved by omitting difficult notes, and (iii) refines a frozen dynamics-aware base policy with an alpha-scaled, finger-only residual that localizes strike-intensity adaptation away from nominal note execution. On EXPRESSIVE-51, a dynamics-rich 51-song subset of RoboPianist, CANTABILE raises Velocity F1 -- jointly measuring pitch, onset, and intensity within a +/-8 MIDI-velocity tolerance -- from 0.06 to 0.34 over the RoboPianist baseline, improves all 51 songs, more than halves matched-note velocity error, and reduces log-mel distance to reference audio by 8%. Intensity-randomized training further enables runtime control of performance intensity without retraining.

</details>


### [231] [UMI-Bridge: Action-Anchored Latent Alignment across Human and Robot Manipulation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18232)
*Haiyi Liu,Jingming Ma,Ke Rui,Yuteng Wei,Yuan Ma,Yushen Zuo,Honglong Tian,Haoran Jia,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Shiyi Chen,Haiyan Mao,Jiaqi Zhang,Chun Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了UMI-Bridge方法，借助UMI作为中介，把人类操作数据和机器人操作进行动作层级对齐，而非单纯依赖视觉特征，大幅提升了机器人学习效率和任务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限于真人在机器人上的操作数据不足，作者希望能利用更多非机器人环境下的人类操作数据（如主观视频、手持UMI录像），但这些数据和机器人操作存在视角、动作表达异构的问题，难以直接迁移。解决这一问题将显著提升机器人学习的实用性和数据利用效率。

Method: 提出UMI-Bridge框架。方法利用UMI动作作为锚点，建立动作等价的跨域对齐。在训练时，先用人类操控数据训练一个双视角的潜在动作模型（LAM），随后冻结其中的“手腕教师”和动力学模型，用于调节后续在UMI和机器人数据上的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的训练。训练中不同数据域通过共享手腕接口实现对齐。

Result: UMI-Bridge在三项真实机器人任务中平均成功率为91.7%，显著优于Naive Co-training（73.3%）。在两个数据效率任务中，仅用25%的机器人数据结合UMI数据，就超过了仅用全部机器人数据的基线方法。另在完全不依赖任务专用机器人演示的情况下，基于UMI的模型在其他两项任务中达到了85%和90%的成功率。

Conclusion: UMI-Bridge通过动作层面对齐，有效利用不同来源的人机操作数据，实现了高效的数据利用与任务迁移，验证了以动作为锚点的潜在表示对齐策略对机器人学习的促进作用。

Abstract: Real-robot demonstrations are limited, motivating the use of human manipulation data collected without robots, including egocentric videos and handheld Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) demonstrations. However, differences in viewpoint, embodiment, and available action supervision make it difficult to align representations across these sources according to manipulation motion rather than visual appearance. We introduce UMI-Bridge, which uses UMI as an intermediate domain to align representations according to action equivalence rather than pixel similarity. UMI action supervision anchors the latent representation to end-effector motion and gripper behavior, while synchronized head-wrist observations and paired ego-UMI clips support alignment across views and domains. We train a dual-view latent action model (LAM) on human manipulation data without robot demonstrations, then freeze its wrist teacher and dynamics model to regularize vision-language-action (VLA) post-training on UMI and robot data. The shared wrist interface enables this training-time supervision across both domains while preserving the policy's standard inference architecture. Across three real-robot tasks, UMI-Bridge achieves 91.7% mean success versus 73.3% for Naive Co-training with matched UMI and robot data. On two data-efficiency tasks, it surpasses a full-data Robot-only baseline using 25% of the robot demonstrations together with UMI data. It also achieves 85% and 90% success on two additional tasks learned from UMI demonstrations without task-specific robot demonstrations. These results support action-anchored latent alignment for data-efficient robot learning and UMI-to-robot task transfer.

</details>


### [232] [ForceDelta-VLA: Distilling Force-Conditioned ActionCorrections for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18242)
*Ju Dong,Yu Fu,Jian Chen,Yimeng Liu,Haocheng Zhao,Lei Zhang,Kaixin Bai,Liding Zhang,Diwen Zheng,Alois Christian Knoll,Angela P. Schoellig,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的ForceDelta-VLA方法，通过将教师模型在有力与无力情况下的预测结合，明确分离出可复用的参考动作与接触修正，从而提升了机器人在复杂力反馈操作任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有力感识别视觉-语言-动作（VLA）方法无法显式区分任务动作与接触修正，演示数据也没有标签标注这种解耦，导致泛化性和灵活性有限需求解决。

Method: 通过冻结的教师模型分别运行结合力输入和不结合力输入的推理，生成参考动作与修正动作目标；引入延时修正以应对状态更新的非同步性；采用异步回放训练，使轻量级策略能够在无需每次重新规划全动作序列的情况下，利用最近的力与状态历史灵活修正动作。

Result: 在九项单臂与双臂复杂接触任务中，ForceDelta-VLA平均成功率为82.2%，相比原始ForceVLA 54.4%有明显提升，教师模型直接执行为70.6%。在成功任务中，平均峰值接触力比ForceVLA降低约26%。

Conclusion: ForceDelta-VLA显式分离参考与修正常数，提升了接触丰富场景下的表现和安全性，为机器人灵巧力控操作提供了更高效与可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Force-aware Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies improve contact-rich manipulation, but typically combine task-level motion and contact-dependent adjustment in a single action prediction. Demonstrations provide no explicit labels for decomposing that prediction into a reusable reference action and a correction. We present ForceDelta-VLA, a correction-distillation framework that constructs an explicit force-correction target using paired predictions from a frozen teacher's force-conditioned and learned force-agnostic modes. A separate delay-correction target accounts for reference-action mismatch and the change in reference state. Training uses asynchronous schedule replay with the cached task context available during execution. The resulting lightweight policy adjusts the reference actions using recent force history and robot state, responding to contact changes between reference-action updates without regenerating complete action chunks. Across nine single-arm and bimanual contact-rich tasks, ForceDelta-VLA achieves an 82.2% mean success rate, compared with 54.4% for the original ForceVLA baseline. Direct execution of our Stage-1 Temporal Teacher achieves 70.6%. Relative to ForceVLA, the complete system reduces mean peak contact force over successful trials by approximately 26% on both platforms.

</details>


### [233] [Acting in Meters: Learning Metric Interactions for Precise Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18243)
*Lijie Wang,Zheng Lu,Yiming Wang,Heyang Yu,Kenghou Hoi,Bowen Hu,Di Cui,Tianyu Xin,Haoran Liao,Wanqi Zhong,Xingjie Fan,Yizhao Xu,Ziliang Wang,Fei Gao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种度量交互框架，结合空间度量信息和语义信息以提升机器人操作的准确性，在多个数据集上显著提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）和世界-动作模型（WAM）虽推动了机器人操作的发展，但未显式建模动作、物体和场景几何之间的度量关系，而人类操作中语义和空间反馈协同起作用。需构建更物理直观的机器人操作表征。

Method: 提出了基于度量空间的对象级与场景级交互建模。对象级上，使用Interaction-Centric Tokens（ICTs）以操作对象为参照显式表征末端执行器运动轨迹，实现结合动作的物理监督；场景级上，设计Metric Action Interaction Field（MAIF）模块，将动作与ICT作为查询去关注点云空间特征，并学习基于几何特性的动作修正。训练上采用两阶段适应，参数和训练量增加很小。

Result: 实验在LIBERO和RoboTwin~2.0数据集上测试，成功率平均分别提升了0.80和3.59个百分点；在现实世界与分布外任务上成功率分别提升了6.80和7.45个百分点。

Conclusion: 度量交互建模（ICT+MAIF）可显著提升机器人操控的空间精准度和泛化能力，且训练与部署成本较低，为机器人智能操控提供了更物理直观的方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models and World-Action Models have advanced language-conditioned robotic manipulation, yet often leave metric relations among actions, objects, and scene geometry implicit. Human manipulation combines semantic understanding of task-relevant objects with spatial feedback that guides hand motion relative to objects and their surroundings. Inspired by this, we introduce a metric interaction framework that models object-level and scene-level interactions in physical Cartesian space at a shared metric scale. At the object level, Interaction-Centric Tokens (ICTs) explicitly represent end-effector pose trajectories relative to manipulated objects and are jointly denoised with actions, providing physically grounded interaction supervision. At the scene level, the Metric Action Interaction Field (MAIF) uses action and ICT queries to attend to metric scene point-cloud features and learns geometry-conditioned action corrections. Through two-stage adaptation, our framework improves diverse VLA and WAM baselines with a small number of additional parameters and training steps. Experiments demonstrate average success-rate gains of 0.80 and 3.59 percentage points on LIBERO and RoboTwin~2.0, respectively, alongside gains of 6.80 percentage points on real-world tasks and 7.45 percentage points on their out-of-distribution variants.

</details>


### [234] [A3P5 NEMESIS Integrated Rover Design for Environmental Reconnaissance and Robotic Sampling with Reproducible Mobility Analysis and an External Data Machine Learning Calibration Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18245)
*Shafi Bin Sultan,Sabik Bin Sultan,Safwan Sadad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为A3P5 NEMESIS的四轮探测车原型，具有远程巡视、环境观测及轻量级操作能力，呈现其系统设计、仿真分析，以及对相关文献的调研。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺少能同时实现远程监测和环境操作的轻量级多功能平台，因此研究旨在开发一种集成多种功能且具有工程可追溯性的服务型遥控探测车。

Method: 采用照片约束的几何重建方法，结合检索得到的5000余份文献资料中的关键研究和技术文档，设计车辆结构并通过静态和动力学分析预测性能。此外，使用公开数据集，通过岭回归对外部传感器进行了精度评估。

Result: 在设计场景下，探测车每个轮子的变速箱输出力矩为3.28牛·米；2kg前部负载显著降低了前翻安全角度。岭回归对传感器评估的CO预测均方根误差为0.502 mg/m³，95%置信区间为0.435-0.569。

Conclusion: 本文建立了探测车原型的系统设计与评估框架，明确了关键测量、控制接口和任务验证需求，尽管实际集成野外性能尚未验证，但为未来实测与完善提供了工程基础。

Abstract: A3P5 NEMESIS is a four-wheel rover intended to combine remote inspection, environmental observation and lightweight manipulation within one serviceable platform. This study develops a photo-constrained geometric reconstruction, a subsystem architecture and a reproducible analytical assessment while distinguishing physical prototype evidence from proposed functions. An exploratory search retrieved 5,000 bibliographic records across ten queries, yielding 4,897 distinct DOI records and 1,212 metadata candidates; selected primary studies and technical documents informed the design. The reconstructed configuration retains the carbon-pattern enclosure, independently steered wheel assemblies, folded manipulator, inclined camera mast and side sampling equipment. A declared 24 kg scenario predicts 3.28 newton-metres of gearbox-output torque per wheel on a 20-degree grade under equal load sharing; a separate static model shows how a 2 kg forward payload reduces the geometric front-tipping bound from 38.1 degrees to 32.7 degrees. These are design screens, not measured operating limits. A public-data calibration benchmark uses 7,344 eligible hourly observations, eight sensor/environmental predictors and chronological training, validation and test partitions. Validation-selected ridge regression achieves a held-out CO root-mean-square error of 0.502 milligrams per cubic metre, with a 95% daily-block bootstrap interval of 0.435-0.569 milligrams per cubic metre. This result concerns an external sensor array and cannot establish NEMESIS accuracy. The combined analysis identifies priority measurements, proposed control interfaces and mission-specific validation requirements. The contribution is a traceable engineering design study and evaluation framework for a prototype whose integrated field performance remains to be established.

</details>


### [235] [${M}^2$Tok: Multi-head Multi-codebook Discrete Action Tokenization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18259)
*Chunpu Xu,Zhixuan Liang,Yuhao Zhang,Chi-Min Chan,Jessie Wang,Yang Xiao,Mengkang Hu,Xiaokang Yang,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种新型多头多码本动作分词器（M^2Tok），大幅降低了动作信号离散化过程的重建损失，从而提升了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作离散化方法重建损失大，不能很好地还原细粒度动作动态，进而限制了下游VLA模型的性能。这种离散化瓶颈成为多模态自回归处理中的主要障碍。

Method: 提出M^2Tok：将潜在动作特征分解为多个head，每个head分配一个独立的码本进行量化，通过多个码本的组合大幅增强分词器的表达能力。

Result: 在RoboTwin、Simpler-Env与3个零样本真实任务中，M^2Tok展示出更低的重建损失和显著更高的VLA模型成功率。消融实验也验证了多头与多码本结构的有效性。

Conclusion: M^2Tok有效突破了动作离散化瓶颈，不仅实现了更精准的动作重建，也提升了VLA模型的下游控制性能，未来可广泛应用于多模态自治智能体。

Abstract: Recent advancements have successfully adapted autoregressive language models to process multimodal signals, such as images and actions. Since raw action signals are continuous, effective tokenization is essential to map high-dimensional inputs into compact discrete tokens for autoregressive processing. However, existing discrete action tokenizers often suffer from high reconstruction loss, failing to preserve the fine-grained dynamics required for precise control. This ``discretization bottleneck'' significantly limits the performance ceiling of downstream Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. To address this, we propose $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok, a Multi-head Multi-codebook Action Tokenizer designed to minimize reconstruction error and enhance policy performance. Our approach introduces two key structural innovations: (1) we decompose the latent action features into multiple heads, enabling the model to implicitly align specific heads with distinct action dimensions; (2) we assign independent codebooks to each head for quantization. By leveraging the combinatorial nature of multiple codebooks, we significantly expand the representational expressivity of the tokenizer, leading to substantially lower reconstruction loss compared to previous methods. We evaluate the $\mathcal{M}^2$Tok-based VLA on the RoboTwin, Simpler-Env, and 3 zero-shot real-world tasks. Experimental results demonstrate our method not only achieves superior reconstruction fidelity but also significantly boosts the success rate of VLA models. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the multi-head and multi-codebook mechanisms. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/cpaaax/M2Tok}{https://github.com/cpaaax/M2Tok}.

</details>


### [236] [Function-Preserving Data Generation for Zero-Shot Real-to-Sim-to-Real Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18293)
*Tianyi Xiang,Xupeng Xie,Jiahang Cao,Andrew F. Luo,Haoang Li,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的Real-to-Sim-to-Real框架，通过形状约束变形和物理一致的任务迁移，实现了无需大量真实数据即可扩展机器人学习，尤其适用于需要精确接触的复杂任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人学习中，大量收集真实世界数据成本高，利用机器人生成数据成为趋势。然而，在涉及复杂接触的任务中，标准的几何增强方法容易破坏关键接触界面，导致数据物理失效，影响最终下游学习效果。

Method: 本方法基于重建物体资产，无需操作员遥控数据，通过受约束的网格变形进行几何增强，物理一致地转移任务姿态和碰撞代理。同时，仿真过程中施加视觉域随机化，保证策略在未经微调的现实情况下依然表现鲁棒。

Result: 实验表明，方法在真实与仿真环境下，对于未见过的物体形状和多样的视觉条件下（特别是复杂接触和长时任务场景），均能实现鲁棒泛化和零样本策略部署。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了几何增强破坏任务关键界面的问题，实现了联系人任务的大规模机器人学习，具有实用推广价值。

Abstract: Robotic data generation is a promising paradigm for scaling robot learning without collecting large-scale real-world data. However, generating geometrically diverse yet physically valid data for contact-rich tasks remains challenging, especially when success depends on precise geometric interfaces. Standard shape augmentation methods often distort task-critical interfaces, resulting in invalid contact relationships, e.g., fit mismatches or interpenetration, rendering downstream interactions infeasible. To address these limitations, we propose a function-preserving Real-to-Sim-to-Real framework that generates synthetic demonstrations from reconstructed assets without teleoperated source trajectories. Our method augments task-relevant object geometries through constraint-guided mesh deformation, together with physically consistent transfer of task poses and collision proxies. Visual domain randomization is further applied during simulation rollouts, enabling robust zero-shot policy deployment without real-world fine-tuning. Extensive experiments in both real-world and simulation settings demonstrate that our method enables robust generalization across unseen object geometries and diverse visual conditions in contact-rich and long-horizon tasks. Our method provides a practical path toward scalable robot learning for contact-rich tasks via shape deformation.

</details>


### [237] [RAFAIL: Relationship-Aware Failure Detection for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18324)
*Loris Schneider,Edgar Welte,Rania Rayyes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 RAFAIL 的方法，通过关注任务相关关系来检测机器人操作中的异常，更精确地判断执行失败，且在真实场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作在执行过程中，可靠地检测到失败非常重要。然而，传统视觉-语言模型（VLM）虽然能理解任务结果，但运行时开销较高；而普通的异常检测方法又容易被与任务无关的场景变化干扰，即误报。因而需要一种既高效又准确的失败检测方案。

Method: RAFAIL 首先通过 VLM 在离线阶段对成功的任务演示进行标注，识别任务关键进展和重要关系（如夹爪与物体的关系），并基于点云数据学习相应的关系表示。在线时，系统无需 VLM 推理，通过关系特定的 OOD 检测器，仅就这些关键关系进行异常检测，排除场景中无关变化带来的干扰，从而高效检测到任务执行失败。

Result: RAFAIL 在三个真实的机器人操作任务上达到了 73.4% 的平衡准确率，超越了现有最优的 OOD 与不确定性方法基线，并且无需使用失败样本即可实现。

Conclusion: RAFAIL 利用任务相关性关注点与关系表示，更高效、准确地检测机器人操作失败，有效克服了传统 OOD 检测对无关场景变化敏感的问题，实际效果优于传统方法。

Abstract: Detecting failures during execution is essential for reliable robotic manipulation. Vision-language models (VLMs) can assess task outcomes semantically but add runtime computation, whereas out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors may respond to harmless scene variations rather than failure-relevant deviations. We introduce RAFAIL, a framework for detecting execution failures during robotic manipulation. RAFAIL identifies failures by detecting anomalies in task-relevant relationships between entities, such as a gripper and an object or an object and its target. By focusing OOD detection on relevant parts of the observation, RAFAIL reduces sensitivity to task-irrelevant scene variation. Offline, a VLM annotates successful demonstrations with task progress and relationship importance, which are used to learn point-cloud-based relationship representations without relying on policy-internal features. At runtime, relationship-specific OOD detectors evaluate these representations while relationship importance and task progress are predicted without VLM inference. RAFAIL requires no failure data and achieves 73.4% balanced accuracy across three real-world robotic manipulation tasks, outperforming the strongest evaluated OOD- and uncertainty-based baselines.

</details>


### [238] [UAVs Meet Embodied Intelligence: Bridging Human Intents and Flying Dynamics Via Harnessing Physical-Digital AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18326)
*Yonglin Tian,Weiyi Wang,Houhua Lu,Xinyi Li,Yihao Wu,Jingyang Chen,Jianli Sun,Chengxiang Li,Yinuo Chen,Fei Lin,Tengchao Zhang,Jing Yang,Deyi Ji,Jian Di,Naiqi Wu,Yisheng Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了无人机（UAV）领域从传统任务型感知与控制向兼具感知、推理、具身智能和复杂任务安排的新范式转变，并提出了UAV具身智能（UAV EI）及其架构，系统梳理了UAV具身智能相关的最新进展和未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型、世界模型和AI智能体的发展，无人机在感知、推理和复杂行为组织等方面能力日益增强，已突破传统单一任务执行限制，亟需有统一框架梳理该领域新范式和挑战。

Method: 作者提出了5+5分析框架，从五个能力维度描述UAV具身智能（EI），并通过五个体系结构层次分析具体的具身智能无人机（EI UAV），对近期相关领域进展进行了系统性回顾和分类。

Result: 论文系统地回顾了具身形态、具身感知、世界模型、具身规划、视觉语言导航、具身操作和协作等方面的最新研究，同时归纳总结了当前面临的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 作者认为，持续融合物理感知、数字智能和反馈，将推动无人机具身智能进一步自适应和进化，并为实现更高级别的无人机自主智能指出了系统性的发展路径。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) extend embodied intelligence into continuous three-dimensional space, where perception, reasoning, physical embodiment, and action are tightly coupled through flight and environmental interaction. Recent advances in foundation models, world models, and AI agents are shifting UAV autonomy from task-specific perception and control toward systems that can interpret human intent, understand open environments, reason about physical consequences, and organize complex behaviors under embodiment and flight-dynamic constraints. We characterize this emerging paradigm as UAV embodied intelligence (UAV EI) and distinguish it from its system realization, the embodied-intelligent UAV (EI UAV). To provide a unified view of the field, we introduce a 5+5 framework that describes UAV EI through five capability dimensions and EI UAVs through five architectural layers spanning physical embodiment, general cognition, embodied skills, external interaction, and system harnessing. Based on this framework, we systematically review recent progress in embodied morphology, embodied perception, world models, embodied planning, vision-language navigation, embodied manipulation, and embodied collaboration. We further identify long-horizon autonomy, predictive physical reasoning, test-time skill acquisition, and autonomous capability evolution as key challenges toward more general aerial embodied intelligence. Finally, we argue that harnessing physical-digital AI agents, through persistent coupling of digital intelligence with physical sensing, dynamics, action, and feedback, provides a system-level pathway toward adaptive and continuously evolving UAV autonomy. Project resources are available at our project website and GitHub repository.

</details>


### [239] [GraphPoint: Semantic Entity Graphs and Point Trajectories for Compositional Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18358)
*Kang Luo,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoMani基准和GraphPoint方法，通过更精细的实体语义分解和轨迹预测提升机器人操作任务的泛化能力，特别是在依赖自然语言指令时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略在面对新指令时往往难以泛化，尤其是因训练期间语言与场景强相关导致模型学到静态的视觉-动作映射，难以理解和执行语言指定的新组合任务。

Method: 1) 提出CoMani基准，通过控制初始场景和语义要素变化，测试策略对语言而非视觉捷径的依赖。2) 提出GraphPoint框架，将语义实体图与点式轨迹预测结合，通过区分动作类型和修饰词来组织夹爪和物体，预测未来的夹爪轨迹并转化为具体动作。3) 进展预测机制以指导任务中的子任务切换。

Result: 在CoMani上进行的实验和消融研究表明，GraphPoint方法能实现依赖语言指令的泛化，既能在已学过的子任务上组合实体，也能在新任务中重用已学子任务。

Conclusion: 所提方法显著提升了机器人执行基于语言的新组合操作任务的泛化能力，为实现更灵活的基于指令的机器人操作提供有效工具。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies often struggle to generalize beyond their demonstrations, even when new instructions involve familiar objects and behaviors. When language and scenes are strongly correlated during training, a policy can learn a fixed visual-action mapping rather than respond to the requested behavior. We investigate compositional reuse at two levels: within a subtask, combining familiar entities, action types, and action modifiers; and across subtasks, reusing learned subtasks in unseen long-horizon tasks. We introduce CoMani, a benchmark with controlled splits for evaluating both capabilities. Matched initial scenes and controlled changes to a single semantic factor encourage reliance on language rather than visual shortcuts. We further propose GraphPoint, which connects semantic entity graphs to geometric control by predicting future gripper point trajectories and converting them into actions using robot geometry. The framework organizes the gripper and objects by semantic roles and conditions their interactions on action types and modifiers, while predicted progress guides transitions during execution. Experiments and ablations on CoMani validate the effectiveness of our method for instruction-dependent generalization at both levels. Code will be released at GraphPoint.

</details>


### [240] [RecMorph: Topology-Guided Spatial Recurrence for Generalized Morphology Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18359)
*Quanrui Rao,Yong Liu,Xueming Xiao,Yingbo Luo,Kun Wu,Zhenyu Xu,Meibao Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的骨架结构感知的时序递归网络RecMorph，有效提升了跨形态机器控制的泛化性、效率与推理速度，并在多种仿真与真实机器人平台上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形态泛化控制器在面对多肢、多结构的机器人时难以高效、协调地处理动作生成和信息传递问题，因此需要新机制来提升跨形态场景的鲁棒性与效率。

Method: RecMorph采用拓扑引导的时序递归架构，将运动链路按深度优先遍历展开为序列，通过双向递归变换实现肢体间通信与信息渐进融合，并结合残差、归一化及通道调制提升深度变换稳定性，在固定宽深度下计算量较低。

Result: 在UNIMAL等五项任务中，RecMorph平均训练性能最优，推理速率最高，能泛化到包含30肢的尚未见过的形态，被迁移到真实四足机器人平台下表现出色，在高摩擦和名义条件下宏平均性能最强，步态误差比专业MLP降43.5%，并40次物理试验零跌倒。

Conclusion: 拓扑驱动的递归架构为通用形态控制任务提供了高效且强泛化力的通信方案，适配多种虚拟及真实形态，展现实际落地潜力。

Abstract: Generalized morphology control requires a single policy to transform information across limbs with different physical roles, coordinate whole-body motion, and remain efficient as body size grows. Existing communication mechanisms address these requirements only partially. We introduce RecMorph, a topology-guided spatial recurrent architecture that uses recurrent sequence computation to jointly perform cross-limb communication and representation transformation. A depth-first traversal converts the kinematic tree into a morphology-derived sequence, along which shared bidirectional transitions progressively transform limb information before action decoding. Residual preservation, RMS normalization, and input-dependent channel modulation stabilize this repeated spatial transformation, yielding linear token complexity at fixed model width and depth. Across five UNIMAL tasks, RecMorph achieves the strongest mean final training performance among the evaluated generalized morphology controllers and the highest measured inference throughput on FT, while generalizing to unseen variations and bodies with up to 30 limbs. We further migrate representative generalized controllers from UNIMAL benchmarks to a four-platform quadruped setting. RecMorph achieves the best macro-averaged performance under nominal and high friction, reduces nominal velocity RMSE by 43.5% relative to specialist MLPs, and one shared policy completes 40 physical Go1/Go2 trials without falls. These results show that topology-guided recurrent transformation provides an effective and efficient communication mechanism for Generalized Morphology Control and remains effective when transferred from procedural bodies to physical robot platforms. Code and experimental resources are publicly available at https://github.com/quanruirao/RecMorph.

</details>


### [241] [Decoupling Vision, Language, and Action for Efficient Multi-Task Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18374)
*Xiatao Sun,Chen Liang,Ziyao Zeng,Qian Wang,Haoyang Zhang,Yue Sun,Qiucheng Li,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的视觉-语言-动作模型结构（DEM），通过将视觉、语言编码器与动作头分离，大幅减少了推理成本，保持甚至超过了当前主流大模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人操控等实际控制场景中，现有的VLA大模型复杂且运行成本高，但这些情况下对大模型的依赖并不总是必要。论文旨在探究使用更轻量级、解耦的视觉与语言模块是否能在保持性能的前提下，带来更高效的控制推理效率。

Method: 设计了系统对比实验，固定数据、任务和评测流程，仅更换视觉编码器、语言编码器和动作头，比较了不同组合对性能与效率的影响。提出了Decoupled Embodiment Model（DEM），组合精调过的DINOv3视觉编码器、冻结的NeoBERT语言编码器以及MeanFlow动作头，实现单步高效动作生成。

Result: 在18个仿真操作任务（包含不同语言表述和随机场景）及3个真实机器人任务中，DEM在成功率上与当前SOTA的大模型持平，同时推理速度提高8~17倍，单次推理能耗降低6~15倍。

Conclusion: 在所选任务范围内，现代解耦视觉与语言编码器与高效动作头的组合能够以更低的延迟和能耗代价，实现与甚至超越传统大VLA模型的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models attach an action module to a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with billions of parameters and pay for that backbone at every control step. For a low-level manipulation policy, this cost may be unnecessary: the VLM supplies vision and language embeddings, and recent standalone vision encoders and encoder-only language models now match or exceed large VLMs on visual embedding and language understanding benchmarks. We study this question with a controlled experiment. Holding the demonstrations, the training budget, the tasks, and the measurement platform fixed, we vary the vision encoder, the language encoder, and the action head of a decoupled policy and compare against seven VLA baselines. The study yields the Decoupled Embodiment Model (DEM), which pairs a fine-tuned DINOv3 encoder and a frozen NeoBERT encoder with a MeanFlow head that generates each action chunk in a single forward pass. On 18 simulated manipulation tasks with held-out language paraphrases and randomized scenes, and on three real-robot tasks, DEM achieves observed success comparable to state-of-the-art VLM-backbone policies under our evaluation protocol, while running at eight to seventeen times their inference frequency and drawing six to fifteen times less energy per inference. Within this task scope, modern decoupled components offer a better success--latency--energy trade-off.

</details>


### [242] [DistAL: Distance-based Advantage Learning for VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18392)
*Reece O'Mahoney,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的优势学习方法DistAL，在视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型中通过引入基于嵌入空间距离的奖励函数，显著提升了机器人操控任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA中的优势条件方法多采用简单的成功/失败奖励，无法细致区分任务过程中的不同状态质量，限制了策略优化效果。为获得更细致、有效的奖励信号，作者受启发于OOD检测，希望通过更精细的奖励改进价值函数表现。

Method: 提出Distance-based Advantage Learning(DistAL)，以嵌入空间距离作为奖励，训练出能更好区分状态质量的价值函数，并进一步用于优势条件策略优化。该方法在仿真和真实机器人双手灵巧操作任务上进行验证。

Result: DistAL带来了更有信息量的价值函数，使得下游任务的成功率提升明显。实验证明，无论在模拟环境还是现实机器人操控任务中，DistAL均优于以往简单奖励函数的优势条件方法。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入距离的奖励设计显著提升了VLA系统中的策略学习能力，推进了机器人复杂操作任务的成功率，为后续优势学习技术应用于更复杂情景提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) have trans- formed the field of robotic manipulation in recent years by combining the semantic understanding of LLMs with the precise control of flow-matching policies. Advantage conditioning is a recent technique that iteratively improves VLAs by training a value function on deployment data and using this to train an advantage-conditioned policy. Previous works have only applied simple, low-information success/failure rewards, which leave the value function unable to distinguish states of differing quality beyond how far along the task they appear. Motivated by an exploration of out-of-distribution (OOD) detection methods, we introduce Distance-based Advantage Learning (DistAL), which, by using an embedding space distance as a reward, produces a more informative value function and subsequently a higher downstream task success rate. We validate our method on a series of simulation benchmarks and dexterous bi-manual manipulation tasks on real hardware.

</details>


### [243] [DetAug: Obstacle-Blind Trajectory Augmentation for Zero-shot Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18395)
*Reece O'Mahoney,Moritz Zoellner,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的障碍物无关（obstacle-blind）数据增强方法DetAug，用于提升机器人操作策略在存在障碍物环境下的泛化能力，显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于遥操作大规模数据集的机器人操作策略，训练数据多为无障碍环境下的自由运动轨迹，难以迁移到包含障碍物的测试环境。以往方法要么依赖障碍物已知信息做数据增强，要么只能在推理阶段有限调整，限制实际应用。

Method: DetAug对自由空间数据集中的移动（transit）阶段施加障碍物无关的数据增强方法，并将增强参数作为条件标签保存。推理时，采样一批标签并选择碰撞代价最低的执行轨迹，实现在无障碍信息下的策略泛化。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO基准测试上，DetAug达到比次佳方法高20个百分点的无碰撞成功率，在标签空间上选择比基于指导的同策略提高26个百分点。实际机器人硬件实验中，DetAug在需要大幅绕行任务下也能媲美甚至超越依赖障碍物信息的基线方法。

Conclusion: DetAug无需预知障碍物几何信息，融合数据增强与推理调优两类方法之优点，极大提升了机器人操作策略在复杂动态环境下的泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Policies for robotic manipulation are produced by training on large teleoperated datasets. These datasets typically consist of free-space trajectories, making them difficult to transfer to test-time environments with obstacles. Previous methods for closing this gap have largely fallen into two groups. Dataset augmentation addresses it at training time but needs obstacle geometry in advance, whereas steering an existing checkpoint at inference time avoids that requirement but is limited in flexibility. Our method draws from both areas without inheriting either drawback. DetAug applies an obstacle-blind augmentation scheme to the transit phases of a free-space dataset, leaving object interactions untouched, and records the augmentation parameters as an explicit conditioning label. At inference it samples a batch of labels and executes the trajectory with the lowest collision cost. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark DetAug achieves a collision-free success rate more than 20pp above the next best method, and selecting over the label space outperforms guidance on the same policy by 26pp. On real hardware, inference-time steering methods collapse on tasks requiring large detours, while DetAug matches or exceeds an obstacle-conditioned baseline without ever seeing obstacles in training.

</details>


### [244] [Hardware-Free Robotics Laboratories in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18434)
*Santiago Berrezueta-Guzman,Habiba-Loai Khalil,Andrei Koshelev,Vanesa Metaj,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了MR-Robotics LAB平台，实现了将MATLAB机器人轨迹以真实比例在混合现实中重现，便于学习机器人运动，无需物理硬件。初步评价表明系统易于设置并被学生普遍看好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人教学多基于屏幕模拟，抽象且与自身空间脱钩，而物理硬件又因成本、安全、时间限制难以普及。作者希望通过新的方式让学生在自身环境中更直观地理解机器人运动。

Method: 设计了MR-Robotics LAB平台，包括一个浏览器服务验证和规范MATLAB轨迹数据，并将其转换为JSON格式发布；通过Meta Quest 3和Unity应用在混合现实中真实比例重现机器人轨迹，支持碰撞检测和抓取。进行了工程类学生的小规模测试，主要收集其主观感受。

Result: 学生反馈显示平台设置简单（平均得分4.67/5），多视角观察有助于理解机器人工作空间（4.56/5），83%愿意在机器人入门课程中使用。测试仅记录主观感受，无对照组或学习成果比较。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于提出了从仿真到混合现实的可复用轨迹转化流程，并为无需硬件的机器人可视化在工程教育中提供了设计指导。无与传统桌面仿真的优劣对比。

Abstract: Teaching robotics relies on screen-based simulation, showing robot motion in an abstract coordinate frame rather than at real scale in the learner's own space, while access to physical hardware is limited by cost, safety, and scheduling constraints. We present MR-Robotics LAB, a mixed-reality (MR) platform that replays MATLAB-generated robot trajectories at real scale within the learner's physical environment. A browser-based service validates a MATLAB workspace file (.mat), normalizes units, and publishes a versioned JSON trajectory; a Unity application on a Meta Quest 3 then reproduces the authored joint configurations under position control and replays them at the declared frame rate within a physics-enabled scene that supports collision detection and end-effector grasping. A formative single-group evaluation with engineering students found that participants reported low setup effort (M = 4.67 on a 5-point scale) and perceived support for workspace understanding from multi-viewpoint inspection (M = 4.56), and 83% of participants affirmed their willingness to use the platform in an introductory robotics course. The evaluation instrument records only perceived outcomes, without counterbalancing or a learning measure, so no comparative advantage over desktop simulation is claimed. The contribution is a reusable simulation-to-MR trajectory pathway and design guidance for hardware-free robot visualization in engineering education.

</details>


### [245] [VLM-MPPI: Grounding Natural Language in Behaviorally Diverse Trajectories for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18451)
*Hanbing Zhang,Fangguo Zhao,Zerui Li,Xin Guan,Peng Cheng,Shuo Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层无人机导航框架，实现了自然语言意图与室内复杂环境下可实现飞行行为的对齐。系统结合多模态规划与视觉-语言模型，实现鲁棒的语言驱动飞行控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机导航方法难以实现对抽象自然语言意图的高效理解与执行，特别是在室内复杂环境中。作者希望提升无人机对自然语言任务指令的适应性与安全性。

Method: 系统分为多个层次，采用六个行为条件的模型预测路径积分（MPPI）规划器并行生成不同风格的候选轨迹，通过引入模式特定的引导代价与采样偏置，增强轨迹多样性。所有3D轨迹被映射至无人机第一人称视角的RGB视频上，并结合预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）实现通过自然语言提示选择候选路径，随后低层PID控制器跟踪所选轨迹。

Result: 在NVIDIA Isaac Sim仿真以及真实四旋翼平台实验中，系统展现了有语义差异的行为多样性，视觉-语言模型具备较强的语言对齐能力，即便存在响应延迟。所有测试场景均达到了100%任务成功率，飞行安全且可重复。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够有效将自然语言意图转化为动态可行且多样的无人机飞行行为，具有良好的实际应用前景，在仿真与现实环境中均验证了其鲁棒性和任务完成率。

Abstract: We present a hierarchical UAV navigation framework that aligns natural-language intent with dynamically feasible flight behaviors in cluttered indoor environments. To bridge the gap between abstract semantics and low-level control, we employ a parallelized ensemble of six behavior-conditioned Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) planners. Crucially, by designing mode-specific guiding costs and sampling biases, we induce distinct trajectory modes that converge to unique behavioral means, yielding a compact set of intentionally diverse candidates rather than mere stochastic variations. We project these 3D candidates onto the onboard first-person-view RGB stream, turning language grounding into a visual action selection problem. A pretrained vision--language model (VLM) asynchronously selects the candidate index given the overlaid FPV image and a natural-language prompt, while MPPI replans at 20Hz and a PID-based low-level controller tracks the selected trajectory. We implement the full pipeline in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and on a real-world quadrotor platform equipped with LiDAR and RGB sensing. Experiments in both simulation and real-world flights show semantically meaningful behavior diversity, robust language alignment despite VLM latency, and safe, repeatable flight across all modes, achieving 100% task success in our evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [246] [ForwardDLO: Model-Based Bimanual Shape Matching of Unconstrained Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18455)
*Tim Missal,Berk Guler,Lucas Domingues,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters,Paula Dornhofer Paro Costa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ForwardDLO的高效绳索动态模型，实现了对未固定软线物体（如绳索）形状的精准控制，并在仿真与现实任务中大幅超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前机器人操作中对于绳索等变形线状物体的精确控制依然极具挑战性，尤其是在物体未被固定、机器人可以沿长度任意抓取的情况下，操作空间巨大且动态模型推理成本高，限制了实际规划能力。

Method: 提出ForwardDLO：一种递归的隐空间（latent）动态模型，直接预测绳索每段的位移，只基于观测到的绳索状态，无需显式处理各段间的操作，从而大幅降低批量候选动作的评估开销。

Result: 在实际绳索运动的开环预测任务中，ForwardDLO较其他学习模型的误差最低，超越最强基线13%；在固定时间预算下，ForwardDLO可评估的候选动作数是同等精度模型的8到22倍，并且在实际形状匹配任务中表现相当。在高频控制（30Hz）下的仿真布线任务中，任务成功率高达98%，基线方法仅30%。

Conclusion: ForwardDLO能以低推理成本对未固定绳索进行高精度控制规划，不仅精度优异且候选动作评估能力突出，显著提升了机器人对变形线性物体的操作能力。论文还开源了相关模型、代码与大规模数据集。

Abstract: Ropes, cables, and other deformable linear objects appear in tasks from untangling to cable routing and suturing, yet controlling their shape remains a challenge in robot manipulation. We study model-based shape control in a general setting: the object lies unfixated on a support surface and two arms may grasp and move it anywhere along its length. Because each arm chooses a grasp point, direction, and magnitude, the joint action space is combinatorially large, and the dynamics model's per-prediction cost bounds how much of it a planner can search. We present ForwardDLO, a recurrent latent dynamics model for this unfixated bimanual setting that predicts per-segment displacements grounded in the observed rope state at every step. Our model reaches accuracy comparable to more expensive baselines while containing no explicit segment-to-segment operations, which makes batched evaluation of candidate actions cheap. On open-loop prediction of real rope motion it reaches the lowest error of the learned models we evaluate, 13% below the strongest baseline. Within a fixed time budget it scores 8 to 22 times more candidate actions than models of comparable accuracy while matching them in real-world shape matching; and on a simulated routing task at a 30Hz control rate, this throughput converts into 98% task success versus at most 30% for the baselines at their own budgets. We release the model, code, and a dataset of 2.42 million simulated and 14,107 real rope transitions at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ForwardDLO/

</details>


### [247] [Real-Time Bounded Catenary Solver for UAV Tether Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18482)
*Max Beffert,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效且有收敛性保证的非定常系留多旋翼无人机系留索气动阻力仿真数值解法，将求解时间大大降低到微秒级，可在线部署在嵌入式设备上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系留建模方法虽速度较快，但未能提供最坏情形下的收敛或解的存在唯一性等理论保证，难以适应嵌入式及实时应用的严格算时要求。

Method: 将原有求解器转化为含一个未知数的超越方程，推导解析包围区间并证明单调性、凸性与根的唯一性，结合两阶段初值估计和专门化的混合根查找算法优化迭代效率。

Result: 新方法平均（最坏）解算时间分别为6.9μs和7.7μs，相较优化前快40倍，且与原结果高度一致（相对偏差8.7e-9）；在ArduPilot实测部署平均解算0.74ms，完全满足实时和嵌入式需求。

Conclusion: 所提重构算法极大提升了系留无人机建模的实时性和可靠性，并无需对物理模型本身进行任何更改，适合嵌入式资源受限场景部署。

Abstract: For non-stationary tethered multirotor UAVs in real-world conditions, simulating the forces imposed on the drone by the aerodynamic drag of the tether becomes crucial, with online use cases placing a hard bound on the maximum solve time. In previous work, a quasi-analytical catenary tether model reached a mean solve time of 0.51 ms using a general-purpose root finder, but without any worst-case guarantees or proven convergence. In this work, we reformulate the inner solver by reducing the catenary boundary-value problem to a single transcendental equation in one well-conditioned unknown. We derive a closed-form bracket and prove monotonicity and convexity as well as existence and uniqueness of the root, which together guarantee convergence of the solver. We further propose a two-regime initial guess which approximates the true root within 3.4% and reduces the mean iteration count by 68.0% to 2.36 compared to the textbook initialization. Building on the hybrid root-finding method rtsafe (Newton-Raphson with bisection fallback giving bounded iteration counts), we implement a specialized variant that exploits the problem structure to omit unnecessary checks while retaining correctness, which gives up to 1.3 times speedup. With the proposed solver the full tether model achieves a nearly constant solve time of 6.9 us on average and 7.7 us at worst, a 40 times speedup over an optimized re-implementation of the previous method, while agreeing with it to a relative deviation of 8.7e-9. Because the reformulation leaves the underlying physical model untouched, the experimental validation of the previous work carries over unchanged. We further demonstrate its suitability for embedded, resource-constrained platforms with a Lua implementation running directly in ArduPilot on a drone's flight controller, where it stays well inside the scheduling budget with a mean solve time of 0.74 ms.

</details>


### [248] [ActionPiece: Rethinking Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18487)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Zhaolong Shen,Xiaopeng Lin,Yichao Du,Zhirui Zhang,Laurence T. Yang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的动作标记器评估指标 PRC，以及一个注重物理关系保真的动作量化新方法 ActionPiece，并在多项任务中显著提升了策略表现和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前动作标记器常用的评估方式（如均方误差）难以全面反映标记器对动作物理关系的保持能力，容易出现解码后动作细节关系失真。需要新的评估标准及标记方法，更好地服务于视觉-语言-动作模型的学习与执行。

Method: 1）提出物理秩一致性（PRC）指标，用于衡量动作标记器在编码/解码后对本地物理距离秩序的保持能力；2）提出ActionPiece方法，通过协同监督表征学习和量化过程，分别对原空间与量化空间中的物理秩关系进行监督和正则，提升动作编码表征的保真度。

Result: 在相同Qwen3-VL-4B训练条件下，ActionPiece在LIBERO环境中达到了94.8%，在未见的LIBERO-Plus上达68.8%，其他环境也取得显著提升。同时消融实验验证了物理秩关系监督对提升PRC和策略成功率的积极作用。

Conclusion: 结合物理关系监督的新型动作标记方法ActionPiece能显著提高模型动作复现保真度和策略成功率，PRC指标为动作量化领域模型间对比提供了更具物理意义的新视角。

Abstract: Action tokenizers play a central role in autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models, determining both the targets for policy training and the executable commands recovered from predicted tokens. Their fidelity is commonly evaluated using pointwise reconstruction metrics such as mean squared error (MSE), yet small individual errors do not fully characterize how faithfully action adjustments across demonstrations are preserved. After compression, similar actions may still cluster around a representative motion, while the adjustments needed for different contexts are diminished, distorted, or even reversed. We introduce physical rank consistency (PRC) to measure how well tokenization preserves local physical distance rankings after reconstruction. Evaluating decoded actions provides a common reference across token vocabularies and decoder architectures, complementing pointwise accuracy with a measure of relational fidelity. We further present ActionPiece, which preserves physical action relationships through joint supervision of representation learning and quantization. Physical rank preservation supervises near-far ordering in encoder and quantized feature distances, while quantization regularization applies the same ordering to codeword assignment distributions. Both objectives augment reconstruction, producing discrete action tokens for standard autoregressive policy learning and execution through a frozen decoder. Under the same Qwen3-VL-4B policy training setup, ActionPiece achieves 94.8% on LIBERO and 68.8% on unseen LIBERO-Plus, with additional evaluations reaching 71.9% on SimplerEnv and 51.5% across VLA-Arena L0-L2. Component ablations show that the two objectives jointly improve PRC and policy success, demonstrating the value of physical relationship supervision for action tokenization.

</details>


### [249] [TAO-Force: Unifying Force-Aware Perception and Fast-Slow Control for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18497)
*Bohan Gan,Xuanzhang Wen,Yongsheng Zhao,Baoping Cheng,Wenhe Jia,Ye Wang,Gongxin Yao,Han Gao,Jingyao Tang,Lei Zhao,Ji Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合力觉感知的新型视觉-语言-动作模型（TAO-Force），显著提升了机器人在高接触性操作任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前主流视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型只基于视觉输入和位置控制，难以应对需要精细力觉感知和动态顺应的高接触性操作。视觉信息对接触发生和力度判断有限，单一位置控制无法对接触变化自适应反应。为此，该文提出同时提升感知和控制能力的方案。

Method: 提出TAO-Force框架，结合力觉感知与接触调控执行。方法上：（1）在视觉-语言骨干网络中引入力编码特征调制（F-FiLM），将力传感器编码的信息嵌入冻结预训练视觉-语言模型的特征层，强化接触感知；（2）采用快-慢双支路架构，非接触阶段用慢速位置控制跟踪轨迹，接触阶段自动切换快速顺应力控支路，实时调节交互力度。

Result: 在力觉感知任务和四个真实高接触操作任务上，TAO-Force框架表现优异，验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结合力觉感知和接触调控执行能显著提升VLA机器人模型在高接触性操作中的性能，TAO-Force为下一步多模态机器人操作提供了有力基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance across diverse robotic manipulation tasks, yet their predominantly vision-centric perception and position-controlled execution remain insufficient for contact-rich manipulation. Visual observations alone often provide limited evidence of contact onset and interaction magnitude, while position-control policies cannot respond compliantly to rapidly changing contact dynamics. To bridge both the perception and control gaps, we propose TAO-Force, a force-conditioned VLA framework that combines force-aware policy learning with contact-regulated execution. For force-aware perception, TAO-Force introduces Force-conditioned Feature-wise Linear Modulation (F-FiLM) to inject encoded force feedback into the representations of a frozen pretrained visual-language backbone while preserving its semantic priors. For responsive control, it employs a contact-gated fast-slow architecture, with a slow position-control branch tracking nominal trajectories during non-contact phases and a fast admittance-control branch regulating physical interaction during contact phases. Detailed analyses on a force-perception task and real-world evaluations across four contact-rich manipulation tasks validate the effectiveness and robustness of TAO-Force.

</details>


### [250] [InterMASH: A Unified Geometric Representation for Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18504)
*Xuanze Yang,Yumeng Liu,Haiyang Xin,Changhao Li,Haowei Shen,Kai Xu,Ligang Liu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的统一几何表示方法InterMASH，可同时适用于人手和机械手的抓取建模。该方法用球面锚点和球谐展开，以紧凑形式编码手与物体的局部几何和接触信息，结合Diffusion Transformer生成模型，实现了物理可行且一致性高的抓取结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前人手与机械手的抓取合成缺乏统一的表示方法，且传统依赖的表示方案或信息不全，或计算冗余。该研究旨在打破人手与多类机械手之间的表征壁垒，提升跨形体抓取模型的泛化性和实用性。

Method: 提出InterMASH表示方式，用球面固定锚点及低阶球谐编码手与物体的几何和接触信息，形成token序列。并开创性地提出了一种conditional Diffusion Transformer模型，直接在InterMASH空间内联合生成手的几何和接触。

Result: 在大规模ShadowHand基准上，各项物理可行性指标表现与现有最优方法相当；支持多种手型的联合训练。人手数据跨形体微调能提升机械手的抓取成功率和多样性。

Conclusion: InterMASH为人手与机械手的抓取建模提供了统一的可扩展表示。模型不仅能提升抓取效果，也简化了多种手型下的泛用、交互和转移学习。

Abstract: Grasp synthesis aims to generate stable and physically plausible hand--object interactions, and has become a fundamental problem in both human hand modeling and robotic manipulation. However, a unified representation across human and robotic hands is still lacking, mainly due to differences in hand morphology and surface modeling. Prior methods typically rely on either contact maps or dense implicit descriptors to represent interaction, but these representations are often incomplete or computationally expensive and redundant. We propose InterMASH, a unified geometric representation that establishes cross-embodiment correspondence using sphere-fixed anchors. At each anchor, low-degree spherical harmonics compactly encode local hand geometry, object geometry, and contact, forming an explicit and interpretable token sequence. Building on this natively tokenized structure, we introduce a conditional Diffusion Transformer that operates directly in the proposed InterMASH representation space and jointly generates hand geometry and contact, improving consistency and physical plausibility. Our method achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods on key physical feasibility metrics in a large-scale ShadowHand benchmark, supports joint training across multiple hands, and shows that cross-embodiment fine-tuning with human grasp data can improve robotic grasp success and diversity. Project page is available at https://inter-mash.github.io/.

</details>


### [251] [ActiveScale: Scaling Active Perception for Robots across Model, Data, and Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18514)
*Shuai Zhou,Kaisheng Pang,Wenxuan Song,Wenjie Zhang,Xinhu Zheng,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ActiveScale框架，通过改进模型、数据和硬件，实现机器人在操控任务中的主动感知，提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定视角会导致关键任务信息被遮挡或无法观测，现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型难以应对视角变化和主动感知需求。

Method: 1）模型方面：在VLA中加入历史视频观测和相机视角监督，通过姿态token与轻量化预测头关联多视角观测，实现场景一致性理解。2）数据方面：设计1000小时的以人为主视角和机器人数据作为中间训练任务，让模型适应时序和姿态监督。3）硬件方面：推出支持主动感知与移动操控的AMP机器人平台，可通过单操作员远程演示，收集大量包含视角变换的真实数据。

Result: 实验表明该框架在主动感知任务上成功率提高，消融实验证实相机姿态感知及以人为中心中间训练方案的有效性。

Conclusion: ActiveScale为研究和实践机器人主动感知提供了集成化基础，显著提升了机器人操控任务的场景理解和行动能力。

Abstract: Active perception is essential for robotic manipulation when fixed viewpoints leave task-relevant information occluded or unobserved. However, enabling vision-language-action (VLA) models to reason across changing viewpoints and actively acquire informative observations remains challenging. We present ActiveScale, a framework that advances active perception through coordinated model, data, and hardware designs. Our model augments a VLA with historical video observations and explicit camera-pose supervision, using per-frame pose tokens and a lightweight prediction head to associate observations across viewpoints and support a coherent understanding of the scene. To learn from the camera motion naturally present in human activity, we introduce a scalable human--robot mid-training recipe using 1000 hours of egocentric and robotic data, adapting the model to temporal inputs and pose supervision. We further introduce Active-perception Mobile-manipulation Platform (AMP), a robotic platform that supports active perception and mobile manipulation through single-operator teleoperation, enabling scalable collection of demonstrations that coordinate viewpoint changes and manipulation. Experiments demonstrate improved success rates on active-perception tasks, while ablation studies validate the contributions of camera-pose-aware modeling and egocentric mid-training. Together, these components provide an integrated foundation for studying and developing active perception in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [252] [DynoFluxBench: Benchmarking Kinodynamic Space-Time Planners in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18549)
*Franz Queißner,Andreas Orthey,Wolfgang Hönig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynoFluxBench，这是一个用于比较在已知动态环境中运动规划器性能的基准测试框架，并开发了三种新的规划器进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人运动规划领域，缺乏能同时评估运动学-动力学可行性和动态障碍物规避能力的基准框架，限制了相关算法的发展和比较。

Method: 设计了DynoFluxBench基准测试框架，并开发了三种结合运动学-动力学与时空方法的规划器：ST-Db-RRT、ST-GBRRT和KIST。ST-Db-RRT利用不连续性受限的运动基元加轨迹优化扩展搜索树，KIST和ST-GBRRT则以不同启发式指导方式维护动力学可行的搜索树。论文分析了这些新规划器在动态环境下的概率完备性。

Result: 实验证明，ST-Db-RRT比其他方法首次找到解的速度最高可提升32倍；在轨迹优化方法不稳定时，KIST和ST-GBRRT仍具有重要价值。

Conclusion: DynoFluxBench为运动学-动力学规划器的开发与评估提供了标准平台。三种新规划器展示了各自在动态环境下的独特优势，可为相关领域算法的进一步发展和比较提供基础。

Abstract: Robots that leave structured, static environments must plan motions that are kinodynamically feasible and safe among moving obstacles. However, there are no dedicated benchmark frameworks that combine both aspects. To overcome this, we present DynoFluxBench, a framework to compare kinodynamic planners in known, dynamic environments with unbounded arrival time. To demonstrate its utility and establish strong baselines, we develop three dedicated planners, named ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST, that fuse kinodynamic and space-time methods, covering different kinodynamic search paradigms: ST-Db-RRT expands with randomly selected discontinuity-bounded motion primitives using trajectory optimization, whereas KIST and ST-GBRRT maintain a kinodynamically feasible tree with different heuristic guidance. We analyze the probabilistic completeness guarantees of those new planners in dynamic environments. Finally, we evaluate ST-Db-RRT, ST-GBRRT, and KIST using DynoFluxBench, showing that ST-Db-RRT reaches a first solution up to 32 times faster, while KIST and ST-GBRRT remain valuable where trajectory optimization is fragile. Videos and further analysis can be found at https://dynofluxbench.github.io/dynofluxbench/.

</details>


### [253] [GroundingVLN: Reasoning and Acting with Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18581)
*Kailing Li,Yu Han,Tianwen Qian,Yuqian Fu,Jingyu Gong,Jiangming Shi,Xiaoling Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GroundingVLN，一个通过视觉锚定将推理与行动连接起来的视觉-语言导航新方法，取得了领先的实验结果，并具有极高的数据效率和优秀的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航（VLN）代理在将语义推理与空间执行结合时存在两大难题：中间推理没有明确锚定视觉证据，以及高层决策缺乏精准空间目标难以指导底层运动。而认知科学发现，人类导航是由地标到空间目标分层次进行认知和行动。基于此，作者希望通过视觉锚定机制，使推理与行动层次有效衔接，提高导航代理能力。

Method: 提出GroundingVLN框架，将视觉锚定作为推理与执行的接口。具体做法包括：（1）推理时，持续把任务相关的视觉证据锚定在准确的图像位置；（2）执行时，预测与导航进度相一致的像素级目标，并利用几何规划器转化为原始动作。此外，构建了GroundingCOTVLN-188K数据集，并设计了GEAR（Grounded and Execution-Aware Reinforcement Learning）强化学习算法，对推理与空间决策进行联合优化。

Result: GroundingVLN在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上分别取得69.9%和75.1%的SR（成功率）的最新最好成绩，仅需最强基线0.9%的训练数据。在跨数据集泛化任务上也表现突出，仅用R2R数据训练在RxR-CE上SR达59.9%，比最强基线多20.1%。
